- **gitnexus-knowledge-graph**: MCP-native knowledge graph engine for AI coding agents. Gives Claude Code, Cursor, and other agents full codebase structural awareness so they stop missing dependencies and breaking call chains. Use when vibe coding large projects, refactoring, understanding unfamiliar codebases, or before making changes that could have cascading effects.
